import axios from "axios"

import Login from "./pages/Login"
import Register from "./pages/Register"
import Dashboard from "./pages/Dashboard"
import Users from "./pages/Users"
import Setup from "./pages/Setup"




import Builder from "./pages/Builder";
import Wallboard from "./features/wallboard/Wallboard"
import AssistantMode from "./features/ai/AssistantMode"
import Navbar from "./components/Navbar"

const API = "http://127.0.0.1:8000"

// ✅ AXIOS CONFIG CON TOKEN
const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: API
})

api.interceptors.request.use(config => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("token")


  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  }

  return config
})

export default function App() {

  const [installed, setInstalled] = useState(true)
  const [kiosk, setKiosk] = useState(false)

  const [stats, setStats] = useState({})
  const [jobs, setJobs] = useState({})
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState([])
  const [plugins, setPlugins] = useState([])
  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState([])
  const [market, setMarket] = useState([])

  // ======================
  // KIOSK MODE
  // ======================
  useEffect(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search)

    if (params.get("kiosk")) {
      setKiosk(true)
      document.body.style.margin = "0"
      document.body.style.overflow = "hidden"
      document.body.style.cursor = "none"
    }
  }, [])

  // ======================
  // SETUP CHECK (SAFE)
  // ======================
  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/setup")
      .then(r => setInstalled(r.data?.installed ?? true))
      .catch(() => setInstalled(true)) // fallback
  }, [])

  // ======================
  // DASHBOARD LOADER (SAFE MODE)
  // ======================
  const load = async () => {

    const token = localStorage.getItem("token")
     if (!token) return

    try {
      const safe = async (fn, fallback) => {
        try {
          const r = await fn()
          return r.data
        } catch {
          return fallback
        }
      }

      const [
        s,
        l,
        p,
        sug,
        j
      ] = await Promise.all([
        safe(() => api.get("/dashboard/stats"), {}),
        safe(() => api.get("/dashboard/logs"), { logs: [] }),
        safe(() => api.get("/dashboard/plugins"), { plugins: [] }),
        safe(() => api.get("/dashboard/suggestions"), { suggestions: [] }),
        safe(() => api.get("/jobs/"), {})
      ])

      setStats(s)
      setLogs(l.logs || [])
      setPlugins(p.plugins || [])
      setSuggestions(sug.suggestions || [])
      setJobs(j || {})

    } catch (e) {
      console.log("dashboard load fail", e)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {

  const start = () => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem("token")
    if (!token) return

    load()
    const i = setInterval(load, 5000)
    return () => clearInterval(i)
  }

  // pequeña espera para asegurar login
  setTimeout(start, 300)

}, [])

  // ======================
  // AUTH
  // ======================
  const token = localStorage.getItem("token")

  if (window.location.pathname === "/login") return <Login />
  if (window.location.pathname === "/register") return <Register />

  if (!token) {
    window.location.href = "/login"
    return null
  }

  if (window.location.pathname === "/assistant") return <AssistantMode />
  if (window.location.pathname === "/wallboard") return <Wallboard />
  if (window.location.pathname === "/users") return <Users />
  if (window.location.pathname === "/builder") return <Builder />

  if (!installed) return <Setup />

  return <Dashboard />
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import axios from "axios"

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8010",
  headers: {
    "x-api-key": "nexus-secret"
  }
})

export default function ErrorPanel(){

  const [errors,setErrors]=useState([])

  useEffect(()=>{

    const load = async ()=>{
      const r = await api.get("/system/errors")
      setErrors(r.data)
    }

    load()

    const i = setInterval(load,3000)

    return ()=>clearInterval(i)

  },[])

  return(
    <div style={styles.wrap}>

      <h3>Top Errors</h3>

      {errors.length===0 && (
        <div style={styles.ok}>
          No errors detected
        </div>
      )}

      {errors.map((e,i)=>(
        <div key={i} style={styles.row}>
          <span>{e.error}</span>
          <span style={styles.count}>{e.count}</span>
        </div>
      ))}

    </div>
  )
}

const styles={

  wrap:{
    background:"rgba(255,255,255,.05)",
    borderRadius:14,
    padding:14,
    color:"white"
  },

  ok:{
    opacity:.6
  },

  row:{
    display:"flex",
    justifyContent:"space-between",
    marginBottom:6,
    fontSize:13
  },

  count:{
    background:"#e74c3c",
    padding:"2px 8px",
    borderRadius:8,
    fontSize:11
  }

}

export default function Navbar() {

  const user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("user") || "{}");

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex",
      justifyContent: "space-between",
      padding: 15,
      background: "#111",
      borderBottom: "1px solid #222"
    }}>

      <div>
        <b>Nexus</b>
      </div>

      <div>
        <a href="/" style={{ marginRight: 15 }}>Dashboard</a>
        <a href="/users" style={{ marginRight: 15 }}>Usuarios</a>
        <a href="/builder" style={{ marginRight: 15 }}>Builder</a>
        <span style={{ marginRight: 15 }}>{user.username}</span>
        <button onClick={()=>{
          localStorage.clear()
          window.location.href = "/login"
        }}>
          Logout
        </button>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/features/ai/AssistantMode.jsx
===========================================================================


import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import Presence from "../presence/Presence"

export default function AssistantMode(){

  const [expanded,setExpanded]=useState(false)
  const [mood,setMood]=useState("idle")

  /* ===============================
     HOTKEY
  =============================== */

  useEffect(()=>{

    const handler=(e)=>{
      if(e.ctrlKey && e.code==="Space"){
        setExpanded(v=>!v)
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener("keydown",handler)
    return ()=>window.removeEventListener("keydown",handler)

  },[])


  /* ===============================
     WEBSOCKET SISTEMA
  =============================== */

  useEffect(()=>{

    const ws = new WebSocket("ws://127.0.0.1:8000/ws")

    ws.onmessage = (e)=>{

      const msg = JSON.parse(e.data)

      if(msg.type==="job_failed"){
        setMood("error")
      }

      if(msg.type==="job_done"){
        setMood("happy")
      }

      if(msg.type==="job_start"){
        setMood("thinking")
      }

      // volver a idle después de 2s
      setTimeout(()=>setMood("idle"),2000)
    }

    ws.onerror = ()=>console.warn("WS Assistant desconectado")

    return ()=>ws.close()

  },[])


  return(
    <div style={styles.wrapper}>

      <Presence mood={mood}/>

      {expanded && (
        <div style={styles.panel}>
          <h3>Nexus</h3>
          <p>Modo asistente activo</p>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  )
}

const styles={
  wrapper:{
    width:"100vw",
    height:"100vh",
    display:"flex",
    alignItems:"center",
    justifyContent:"center",
    background:"transparent"
  },
  panel:{
    position:"absolute",
    bottom:10,
    width:280,
    background:"rgba(20,20,25,.95)",
    padding:16,
    borderRadius:16,
    color:"white"
  }
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/features/ai/BrainMap.jsx
===========================================================================


import ReactFlow, { Background, Controls } from "reactflow"
import "reactflow/dist/style.css"

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import axios from "axios"

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8010",
  headers: { "x-api-key": "nexus-secret" }
})

export default function BrainMap(){

  const [nodes,setNodes] = useState([])
  const [edges,setEdges] = useState([])

  useEffect(()=>{

    const load = async ()=>{

      try{

        const goals = await api.get("/goals")
        const jobs = await api.get("/jobs")

        buildGraph(goals.data, jobs.data)

      }catch(e){
        console.error(e)
      }

    }

    load()

    const i = setInterval(load,2000)

    return ()=>clearInterval(i)

  },[])


  function buildGraph(goals,jobs){

    const n=[]
    const e=[]

    let id=1

    goals.forEach((g,i)=>{

      const goalId = "goal_"+id++

      n.push({
        id:goalId,
        position:{x:100,y:i*150},
        data:{label:"🎯 "+g.goal},
        style:{background:"#8e44ad",color:"white"}
      })

      Object.entries(jobs).forEach(([jid,j])=>{

        const jobNode="job_"+jid

        n.push({
          id:jobNode,
          position:{x:400,y:i*150+50},
          data:{label:"⚙ "+j.workflow},
          style:{background:"#2980b9",color:"white"}
        })

        e.push({
          id:"e_"+goalId+"_"+jid,
          source:goalId,
          target:jobNode
        })

      })

    })

    setNodes(n)
    setEdges(e)

  }


  return(

    <div style={{height:"600px",background:"#111"}}>

      <ReactFlow
        nodes={nodes}
        edges={edges}
        fitView
      >

        <Background/>
        <Controls/>

      </ReactFlow>

    </div>

  )

}

ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/features/ai/Copilot.jsx

import { useState } from "react"
import axios from "axios"

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8010",
  headers: {
    "x-api-key": "nexus-secret"
  }
})

export default function Copilot({ onWorkflow }) {

  const [prompt,setPrompt] = useState("")
  const [loading,setLoading] = useState(false)

  const generate = async () => {

    setLoading(true)

    try {

      const r = await api.post("/copilot/generate",{
        prompt
      })

      if(r.data.ok){

        onWorkflow(r.data.workflow)

      }

    } catch(e){
      console.error(e)
    }

    setLoading(false)
  }

  return(

    <div style={styles.box}>

      <h3>Nexus Copilot</h3>

      <input
        placeholder="Describe una automatización..."
        value={prompt}
        onChange={e=>setPrompt(e.target.value)}
        style={styles.input}
      />

      <button onClick={generate} style={styles.btn}>
        {loading ? "Generando..." : "Generar Workflow"}
      </button>

    </div>
  )
}

const styles = {

 box:{
  background:"rgba(255,255,255,.05)",
  padding:14,
  borderRadius:12
 },

 input:{
  width:"100%",
  padding:8,
  marginBottom:8
 },

 btn:{
  width:"100%",
  padding:8
 }

}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/features/jobs/JobTimeline.jsx
===========================================================================


import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import axios from "axios"

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8010",
  headers: {
    "x-api-key": "nexus-secret"
  }
})

export default function JobTimeline({jobId}){

  const [steps,setSteps]=useState([])

  useEffect(()=>{

    if(!jobId) return

    api.get(`/jobs/${jobId}/steps`)
      .then(r=>setSteps(r.data))

  },[jobId])

  return(
    <div style={styles.wrap}>

      <h3>Workflow Timeline</h3>

      {steps.map((s,i)=>(
        <div key={i} style={styles.step}>

          <div style={{
            ...styles.dot,
            background:s.status==="success"
              ? "#2ecc71"
              : "#e74c3c"
          }}/>

          <div style={styles.info}>
            <div>{s.step}</div>
            <div style={styles.time}>{s.timestamp}</div>
          </div>

        </div>
      ))}

    </div>
  )
}

const styles={

  wrap:{
    background:"rgba(255,255,255,.05)",
    padding:16,
    borderRadius:12,
    color:"white"
  },

  step:{
    display:"flex",
    gap:10,
    marginBottom:8
  },

  dot:{
    width:10,
    height:10,
    borderRadius:"50%"
  },

  info:{
    display:"flex",
    justifyContent:"space-between",
    width:"100%"
  },

  time:{
    opacity:.5,
    fontSize:12
  }
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/features/jobs/JobsPanel.jsx
===========================================================================


import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react"
import axios from "axios"
import JobTimeline from "./JobTimeline"

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8010"
})

api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("token")

  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  }

  return config
})

export default function JobsPanel(){

  const [jobs,setJobs]=useState({})
  const [metrics,setMetrics]=useState({})
  const [expanded,setExpanded]=useState(null)
  const [selected,setSelected]=useState(null)

  /* =========================
     polling
  ========================= */

  useEffect(() => {

  const loadInitial = async () => {
    const [jobsRes, metricsRes] = await Promise.all([
      api.get("/jobs"),
      api.get("/metrics")
    ])
    setJobs(jobsRes.data)
    setMetrics(metricsRes.data)
  }

  loadInitial()

  const ws = new WebSocket("ws://127.0.0.1:8010/ws")

  ws.onmessage = (e) => {

    const msg = JSON.parse(e.data)

    if (msg.type === "job_update") {

      setJobs(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        [msg.job_id]: {
          ...prev[msg.job_id],
          status: msg.status,
          result: msg.result
        }
      }))
    }

    if (msg.type === "metrics_update") {
      setMetrics(msg.data)
    }

    if (msg.type === "circuit_update") {
      setMetrics(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        circuit_state: msg.state
      }))
    }
  }

  return () => ws.close()

}, [])

  /* =========================
     ordenar por fecha
  ========================= */

  const orderedEntries = useMemo(()=>{
    return Object.entries(jobs)
      .sort((a,b)=>{
        const da = new Date(a[1].updated_at || 0)
        const db = new Date(b[1].updated_at || 0)
        return db - da
      })
  },[jobs])

  const list = orderedEntries.map(e=>e[1])

  /* =========================
     métricas
  ========================= */

  const stats = useMemo(()=>( {
    total: list.length,
    running: list.filter(j=>j.status==="running").length,
    queued: list.filter(j=>j.status==="queued").length,
    done: list.filter(j=>j.status==="done").length,
    error: list.filter(j=>j.status==="error").length
  }),[jobs])

  /* ========================= */

  const color=(s)=>{
    if(s==="running") return "#f6c344"
    if(s==="done") return "#2ecc71"
    if(s==="queued") return "#3498db"
    if(s==="error") return "#e74c3c"
    return "#999"
  }

  const toggle = (id)=>{
    setExpanded(expanded===id ? null : id)
  }

  const formatResult = (r)=>{
    if(!r) return "Sin resultado"
    if(typeof r === "string") return r
    return JSON.stringify(r,null,2)
  }

  /* ========================= */

  return(
  <div style={styles.wrap}>

    <h3 style={{marginBottom:14,fontSize:18}}>Jobs</h3>

    <div style={styles.stats}>
      <Stat label="Total" value={stats.total}/>
      <Stat label="Running" value={stats.running}/>
      <Stat label="Queued" value={stats.queued}/>
      <Stat label="Done" value={stats.done}/>
      <Stat label="Error" value={stats.error}/>
    </div>

    <div style={styles.list}>

      {orderedEntries.map(([id,j])=>{

        const isExpanded = expanded===id

        return(
          <div key={id} style={styles.rowWrap}>

            <div 
              style={styles.row}
              onClick={()=>toggle(id)}
            >

              <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8}}>

                <span style={styles.arrow}>
                  {isExpanded ? "▾" : "▸"}
                </span>

                <span style={styles.version}>
                  v{j.version}
                </span>

                <span>{j.workflow}</span>

              </div>

              <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10}}>

                {j.updated_at && (
                  <span style={styles.time}>
                    {new Date(j.updated_at).toLocaleTimeString()}
                  </span>
                )}

                <span style={{
                  ...styles.badge,
                  background: color(j.status),
                  animation: j.status==="running"
                    ? "pulse 1.2s infinite"
                    : "none"
                }}>
                  {j.status}
                </span>

              </div>

            </div>

            {isExpanded && (
              <div style={styles.detail}>

                {/* RESULTADO DEL JOB */}

                <pre style={{
                  ...styles.pre,
                  color: j.status==="error"
                    ? "#ff6b6b"
                    : "#cfcfcf"
                }}>
                  {formatResult(j.result)}
                </pre>

                {/* 🔥 WORKFLOW TIMELINE */}

                <div style={{marginTop:10}}>
                  <JobTimeline jobId={id}/>
                </div>

              </div>
            )}

          </div>
        )

      })}

    </div>

  </div>
)
}

  )
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { connectWS } from "../../services/ws";

export default function RealtimePanel({ jobId }) {

  const [logs, setLogs] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    connectWS((msg) => {

      // filtrar por job
      if (jobId && msg.job_id !== jobId) return;

      setLogs(prev => [msg, ...prev].slice(0, 50));
    });
  }, [jobId]);

  return (
    <div style={{
      background: "#000",
      color: "#0f0",
      padding: 10,
      borderRadius: 8,
      marginTop: 20
    }}>
      <h3>Realtime Logs</h3>

      {logs.map((l, i) => (
        <div key={i}>
          [{l.type}] {l.message || ""}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/features/presence/Presence.jsx

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react"
import face from "../../assets/face-mask.png"
import { NexusState } from "../../nexusState"

export default function Presence() {

  const ref = useRef()
  const particlesRef = useRef([])
  const animationRef = useRef()
  const audioLevelRef = useRef(0)

  useEffect(() => {

    const canvas = ref.current
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d")

    /* =========================
       CANVAS SCALE SAFE
    ========================= */

    function resize() {
      const DPR = window.devicePixelRatio || 1

      canvas.width = window.innerWidth * DPR
      canvas.height = window.innerHeight * DPR

      canvas.style.width = "100vw"
      canvas.style.height = "100vh"

      ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0)
      ctx.scale(DPR, DPR)
    }

    resize()
    window.addEventListener("resize", resize)

    /* =========================
       VARIABLES
    ========================= */

    const particles = particlesRef.current
    let mouse = { x: window.innerWidth/2, y: window.innerHeight/2 }
    let t = 0

    /* =========================
       AUDIO REACTIVE
    ========================= */

    navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia({ audio: true })
      .then(stream => {

        const audioCtx = new AudioContext()
        const analyser = audioCtx.createAnalyser()
        const source = audioCtx.createMediaStreamSource(stream)

        analyser.fftSize = 256
        source.connect(analyser)

        const data = new Uint8Array(analyser.frequencyBinCount)

        function readAudio() {
          analyser.getByteFrequencyData(data)
          const avg = data.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / data.length
          audioLevelRef.current = avg / 255
          requestAnimationFrame(readAudio)
        }

        readAudio()

      })
      .catch(() => {
        audioLevelRef.current = 0
      })

    /* =========================
       MOUSE TRACK
    ========================= */

    window.addEventListener("mousemove", e => {
      mouse.x = e.clientX
      mouse.y = e.clientY
    })

    /* =========================
       CARGAR MÁSCARA
    ========================= */

    const img = new Image()
    img.src = face

    img.onload = () => {

      particles.length = 0

      const off = document.createElement("canvas")
      const octx = off.getContext("2d")

      const size = 500
      off.width = size
      off.height = size

      octx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, size, size)
      const data = octx.getImageData(0, 0, size, size).data

      const gap = 4

      for (let y = 0; y < size; y += gap) {
        for (let x = 0; x < size; x += gap) {

          const i = (y * size + x) * 4

          if (data[i + 3] > 120) {

            particles.push({
              x: Math.random() * window.innerWidth,
              y: Math.random() * window.innerHeight,
              tx: window.innerWidth/2 - size/2 + x,
              ty: window.innerHeight/2 - size/2 + y,
              vx: 0,
              vy: 0
            })
          }
        }
      }

      animate()
    }

    /* =========================
       COLOR DINÁMICO
    ========================= */

    function color() {
      switch (NexusState.mode) {
        case "listening": return "rgba(0,200,255,0.9)"
        case "thinking": return "rgba(255,170,0,0.95)"
        case "speaking": return "rgba(124,92,255,1)"
        case "error": return "rgba(255,50,80,0.9)"
        default: return "rgba(124,92,255,0.55)"
      }
    }

    /* =========================
       LOOP PRINCIPAL
    ========================= */

    function animate() {

      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)

      t += 0.02
      const breathe = Math.sin(t) * 2

      ctx.shadowBlur = 24
      ctx.shadowColor = "#7c5cff"

      const mx = (mouse.x - window.innerWidth/2) * 0.03
      const my = (mouse.y - window.innerHeight/2) * 0.03

      for (const p of particles) {

        const dx = p.tx + mx - p.x
        const dy = p.ty + my - p.y

        p.vx += dx * 0.02
        p.vy += dy * 0.02

        const audioBoost = audioLevelRef.current * 8

        p.vx += (Math.random() - 0.5) * audioBoost
        p.vy += (Math.random() - 0.5) * audioBoost

        p.vx *= 0.86
        p.vy *= 0.86

        p.x += p.vx
        p.y += p.vy + breathe

        ctx.fillStyle = color()
        ctx.fillRect(p.x, p.y, 2, 2)
      }

      animationRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate)
    }

    /* =========================
       CLEANUP
    ========================= */

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animationRef.current)
      window.removeEventListener("resize", resize)
    }

  }, [])

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={ref}
      style={{
        position: "fixed",
        inset: 0,
        pointerEvents: "none",
        zIndex: 0,
        opacity: 0.45,
        transition: "opacity .4s ease"
      }}
    />
  )
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import axios from "axios"
import { getAuthHeaders } from "../../services/auth"

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8010",
  headers: { "x-api-key": "nexus-secret" }
})

api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token")

  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  }

  return config
})

export default function MissionControl(){

  const [goals,setGoals] = useState([])
  const [jobs,setJobs] = useState({})
  const [voice,setVoice] = useState([])

  /* =========================
     LOAD DATA
  ========================= */

  useEffect(()=>{

    const load = async ()=>{

      try{

        const g = await api.get("/goals")
        setGoals(g.data)

        const j = await api.get("/jobs")
        setJobs(j.data)

      }catch(e){
        console.error(e)
      }
    }

    load()

    const i=setInterval(load,2000)

    return ()=>clearInterval(i)

  },[])


  /* =========================
     REALTIME VOICE
  ========================= */

  useEffect(()=>{

    const ws = new WebSocket("ws://127.0.0.1:8010/ws")

    ws.onmessage = (e)=>{

      const msg = JSON.parse(e.data)

      if(msg.type === "nexus_voice"){

        setVoice(v => [
          msg.message,
          ...v.slice(0,8)
        ])

      }

    }

    return ()=>ws.close()

  },[])


  /* ========================= */

  const running = Object.values(jobs)
    .filter(j=>j.status==="running").length

  const done = Object.values(jobs)
    .filter(j=>j.status==="done").length


  /* ========================= */

  return(

    <div style={styles.page}>

      <h1 style={styles.title}>Nexus Mission Control</h1>

      <div style={styles.grid}>

        <Card title="Active Goals">

          {goals.map(g=>(
            <div key={g.id} style={styles.row}>
              {g.goal}
            </div>
          ))}

        </Card>


        <Card title="System Health">

          <div style={styles.metric}>
            Running jobs: {running}
          </div>

          <div style={styles.metric}>
            Completed jobs: {done}
          </div>

        </Card>


        <Card title="Nexus Voice">

          {voice.map((v,i)=>(
            <div key={i} style={styles.log}>
              {v}
            </div>
          ))}

        </Card>


      </div>

    </div>
  )
}


/* ========================= */

  return(

    <div style={styles.card}>

      <div style={styles.cardTitle}>
        {title}
      </div>

      {children}

    </div>

  )

}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import axios from "axios"

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8010",
  headers: {
    "x-api-key": "nexus-secret"
  }
})

export default function SystemHealth(){

  const [health,setHealth]=useState(null)

  useEffect(()=>{

    const load = async ()=>{
      const r = await api.get("/system/health")
      setHealth(r.data)
    }

    load()

    const i = setInterval(load,2000)

    return ()=>clearInterval(i)

  },[])

  if(!health) return null

  return(
    <div style={styles.wrap}>

      <h3>System Health</h3>

      <div style={styles.score}>
        {health.health_score}%
      </div>

      <div style={styles.grid}>
        <Stat label="Errors" value={(health.error_rate*100).toFixed(1)+"%"}/>
        <Stat label="Queue" value={health.queue_size}/>
        <Stat label="Circuits" value={health.open_circuits.length}/>
        <Stat label="Jobs" value={health.total_jobs}/>
      </div>

    </div>
  )
}

function Stat({label,value}){
  return(
    <div style={styles.card}>
      <div style={styles.label}>{label}</div>
      <div style={styles.value}>{value}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/features/wallboard/Wallboard.jsx

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react"
import axios from "axios"
import Presence from "../presence/Presence"
import SystemHealth from "../system/SystemHealth"
import ErrorPanel from "../../components/ErrorPanel"

const API = "http://127.0.0.1:8010"

export default function Wallboard(){

  const [jobs,setJobs]=useState({})
  const [time,setTime]=useState(new Date())
  const [events,setEvents]=useState([])
  const [health,setHealth]=useState("healthy")

  /* reloj */
  useEffect(()=>{
    const i = setInterval(()=>setTime(new Date()),1000)
    return ()=>clearInterval(i)
  },[])

  /* bootstrap inicial */
  useEffect(()=>{
    axios.get(`${API}/jobs`,{
      headers:{ "x-api-key":"nexus-secret" }
    }).then(r=>setJobs(r.data))
  },[])

  /* websocket realtime */
  useEffect(()=>{

  const ws = new WebSocket("ws://127.0.0.1:8010/ws")

  ws.onmessage = (e)=>{

    const msg = JSON.parse(e.data)

    if(msg.type==="job_done"){
      pushActivity("He completado una automatización sin incidentes.")
      setCognitiveState("ÓPTIMO")
    }

    if(msg.type==="job_error"){
      pushActivity("He detectado una anomalía operativa. Estoy evaluando el impacto.")
      setCognitiveState("ANALIZANDO")
    }

    if(msg.type==="circuit.opened"){
      pushActivity("He activado un protocolo de protección preventiva.")
      setProtection("PROTECCIÓN ELEVADA")
      setCognitiveState("DEFENSIVO")
    }

    if(msg.type==="circuit.closed"){
      pushActivity("He restablecido condiciones estables.")
      setProtection("ACTIVA")
      setCognitiveState("ÓPTIMO")
    }

    if(msg.type === "nexus_decision"){
      console.log("Nexus decision:", msg)
    }

    if(msg.type === "nexus_voice"){
    setEvents(ev => [
    msg.message,
    ...ev.slice(0,10)
    ])
}

  }

  return ()=>ws.close()

},[])

  const pushEvent = (text)=>{
    setEvents(ev=>[
      {
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),
        text
      },
      ...ev.slice(0,8)
    ])
  }

  /* métricas derivadas */

  const list = Object.values(jobs)

  const stats = useMemo(()=>({

    total: list.length,
    running: list.filter(j=>j.status==="running").length,
    queued: list.filter(j=>j.status==="queued").length,
    done: list.filter(j=>j.status==="done").length,
    error: list.filter(j=>j.status==="error").length

  }),[jobs])

  const healthText = {
    healthy:"🟢 Sistema saludable",
    warning:"🟡 Atención requerida",
    critical:"🔴 Acción necesaria"
  }

  return(
    <div style={styles.page}>

      <Presence />
      <SystemHealth/>
      <ErrorPanel/>

      <div style={styles.header}>
        <div style={styles.brand}>NEXUS CLOUD</div>
        <div style={styles.clock}>{time.toLocaleTimeString()}</div>
      </div>

      <div style={styles.systemHealth}>
        {healthText[health]}
      </div>

      <div style={styles.metrics}>

        <Metric title="Automatizaciones" value={stats.total}/>
        <Metric title="En ejecución" value={stats.running} color="#c7a73b"/>
        <Metric title="En espera" value={stats.queued} color="#3b82f6"/>
        <Metric title="Completadas" value={stats.done} color="#3ba776"/>
        <Metric title="Errores" value={stats.error} color="#c75c5c"/>

      </div>

      <div style={styles.activity}>

        <div style={styles.activityTitle}>Actividad reciente</div>

        {events.map((e,i)=>(
          <div key={i} style={styles.logRow}>
            <span style={styles.logTime}>{e.time}</span>
            <span style={styles.logText}>{e.text}</span>
          </div>
        ))}

      </div>

    </div>
  )
}

/* ========================= */

function Metric({title,value,color}){

  return(
    <div style={styles.metric}>
      <div style={styles.metricTitle}>{title}</div>
      <div style={{
        ...styles.metricValue,
        color: color || "#f5f5f7"
      }}>
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/features/workflows/Workflow.jsx

import ReactFlow, {
  addEdge,
  Background,
  Controls
} from "reactflow"
import "reactflow/dist/style.css"

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react"
import toast from "react-hot-toast"

import Presence from "../presence/Presence"
import ConditionNode from "../../nodes/ConditionNode"
import Copilot from "../ai/Copilot"

import axios from "axios"

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:8010",
  headers: { "x-api-key": "nexus-secret" }
})

export default function Workflow() {

  const [logs, setLogs] = useState([])
  const [activeAction, setActiveAction] = useState(null)
  const [finishedActions, setFinishedActions] = useState([])
  const [errorAction, setErrorAction] = useState(null)
  const [aiPrompt,setAiPrompt] = useState("")

  const idRef = useRef(2)

  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState([
    {
      id: "1",
      position: { x: 100, y: 100 },
      data: { label: "▶ Trigger" },
      type: "default"
    }
  ])

  const [edges, setEdges] = useState([])

  const nodeTypes = { condition: ConditionNode }

  /* ==================================================
     WEBSOCKET LIVE EVENTS
  ================================================== */

  useEffect(() => {

    const ws = new WebSocket("ws://127.0.0.1:8010/ws")

    ws.onmessage = (e) => {

      const msg = JSON.parse(e.data)

      setLogs(l => [...l.slice(-120), msg])

      if (msg.type === "workflow_step") {
        setActiveAction(msg.action)
        setErrorAction(null)
      }

      if (msg.type === "workflow_result") {
        setFinishedActions(prev => [...prev, msg.action])
        setActiveAction(null)
      }

      if (msg.type === "workflow_error") {
        setErrorAction(msg.action)
        setActiveAction(null)
      }

    }

    return () => ws.close()

  }, [])

  /* ==================================================
     VISUAL NODE COLORING
  ================================================== */

  const styledNodes = nodes.map(n => {

    if (!n.data?.action) return n

    let style = {
      border: "1px solid #444",
      borderRadius: 8,
      padding: 6
    }

    if (n.data.action === activeAction) {
      style = {
        ...style,
        border: "2px solid #f6c344",
        boxShadow: "0 0 12px #f6c344"
      }
    }

    if (finishedActions.includes(n.data.action)) {
      style = {
        ...style,
        border: "2px solid #2ecc71",
        boxShadow: "0 0 12px #2ecc71"
      }
    }

    if (errorAction === n.data.action) {
      style = {
        ...style,
        border: "2px solid #e74c3c",
        boxShadow: "0 0 12px #e74c3c"
      }
    }

    return {
      ...n,
      style
    }

  })

  /* ==================================================
     AI WORKFLOW GENERATION
  ================================================== */

  const generateWithAI = async () => {

    if(!aiPrompt) return

    try{

      const res = await api.post("/ai/generate-workflow",{
        prompt: aiPrompt
      })

      const wf = res.data.workflow

      buildGraphFromWorkflow(wf)

      toast.success("Workflow generado por IA")

    }catch(err){

      toast.error("Error generando workflow")

    }

  }

  /* ==================================================
     BUILD GRAPH FROM WORKFLOW JSON
  ================================================== */

  function buildGraphFromWorkflow(workflow){

    const newNodes=[
      {
        id:"1",
        position:{x:100,y:100},
        data:{label:"▶ Trigger"}
      }
    ]

    const newEdges=[]

    let lastId="1"

    workflow.steps.forEach((step,i)=>{

      const id=String(i+2)

      newNodes.push({
        id,
        position:{x:300,y:100+i*120},
        data:{
          label:step.action,
          action:step.action
        }
      })

      newEdges.push({
        id:lastId+"-"+id,
        source:lastId,
        target:id
      })

      lastId=id

    })

    setNodes(newNodes)
    setEdges(newEdges)

  }

  /* ==================================================
     ADD NODE
  ================================================== */

  const addNode = (action) => {

    const newNode = {
      id: String(idRef.current++),
      position: { x: 260, y: 150 + idRef.current * 70 },
      data: { label: action, action },
      type: "default"
    }

    setNodes(n => [...n, newNode])

  }

  /* ==================================================
     SAVE WORKFLOW
  ================================================== */

  const saveWorkflow = async () => {

    const nodeMap = {}
    nodes.forEach(n => nodeMap[n.id] = n)

    const edgeMap = {}
    edges.forEach(e => {
      if (!edgeMap[e.source]) edgeMap[e.source] = []
      edgeMap[e.source].push(e)
    })

    function buildTree(nodeId) {

      const node = nodeMap[nodeId]
      if (!node) return null

      if (node.data?.action) {

        const children = (edgeMap[nodeId] || [])
          .map(e => buildTree(e.target))
          .filter(Boolean)

        return {
          type: "action",
          action: node.data.action,
          value: null,
          next: children
        }

      }

      return null

    }

    const targets = edges.map(e => e.target)
    const rootNode = nodes.find(n => !targets.includes(n.id))

    if (!rootNode) {
      toast.error("No se detecta nodo raíz")
      return
    }

    const tree = buildTree(rootNode.id)

    if (!tree) {
      toast.error("Workflow vacío o mal conectado")
      return
    }

    await api.post("/workflows/save", [
      {
        name: "workflow-1",
        trigger: "manual",
        steps: [tree]
      }
    ])

    toast.success("Workflow guardado ✓")

  }

  /* ==================================================
     RUN WORKFLOW
  ================================================== */

  const runAsync = async () => {

    setFinishedActions([])
    setErrorAction(null)

    const res = await api.post("/jobs/run", { name: "workflow-1" })

    toast("Job creado: " + res.data.job_id)

  }

  /* ==================================================
     LOAD WORKFLOW FROM COPILOT
  ================================================== */

  const loadGeneratedWorkflow = (workflow) => {

    buildGraphFromWorkflow(workflow)

  }

  /* ==================================================
     UI
  ================================================== */

  return (
    <div style={{ position: "relative" }}>

      <div style={{
        position: "absolute",
        right: 20,
        top: 20,
        width: 140,
        height: 140,
        pointerEvents: "none",
        zIndex: 50
      }}>
        <Presence />
      </div>

      {/* ================= AI PROMPT ================= */}

      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,marginBottom:8}}>

        <input
          value={aiPrompt}
          onChange={e=>setAiPrompt(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Describe el workflow..."
          style={{flex:1,padding:6}}
        />

        <button onClick={generateWithAI}>
          Generate AI
        </button>

      </div>

      {/* ================= ACTION BUTTONS ================= */}

      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, marginBottom: 8 }}>
        <button onClick={() => addNode("list_dir")}>List Dir</button>
        <button onClick={() => addNode("move_files")}>Move Files</button>
        <button onClick={() => addNode("create_folder")}>Create Folder</button>
        <button onClick={saveWorkflow}>Save</button>
        <button onClick={runAsync}>Run Async 🚀</button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= WORKFLOW CANVAS ================= */}

      <div style={{
        height: 600,
        background: "#111",
        borderRadius: 12,
        overflow: "hidden"
      }}>

        <ReactFlow
          nodes={styledNodes}
          edges={edges}
          onConnect={(p) => setEdges(e => addEdge(p, e))}
          nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
          fitView
        >
          <Background />
          <Controls />
        </ReactFlow>

        <Copilot onWorkflow={loadGeneratedWorkflow}/>

      </div>

      {/* ================= LOG PANEL ================= */}

      <div style={{
        marginTop: 10,
        height: 120,
        overflow: "auto",
        background: "#000",
        color: "#0f0",
        fontSize: 12,
        padding: 8,
        borderRadius: 8
      }}>
        {logs.map((l, i) => (
          <div key={i}>{JSON.stringify(l)}</div>
        ))}
      </div>

    </div>
  )
}

import { ACTIONS } from "../config/actions";
import { Handle, Position } from "reactflow";

export default function ActionNode({ data, id }) {

  const action = data.action || "create_folder";

  const config = ACTIONS[action];

  // 🔥 NODE STATE
  const state = data.nodeState || "idle";

  // =========================
  // UPDATE PARAM FIELD
  // =========================
  const updateField = (field, value) => {

    data.onChange(id, {

      ...data,

      params: {

        ...data.params,

        [field]: value
      }
    });
  };

  // =========================
  // CHANGE ACTION
  // =========================
  const changeAction = (newAction) => {

    data.onChange(id, {

      ...data,

      action: newAction,

      params: {}
    });
  };

  // =========================
  // VISUAL STATES
  // =========================
  const styles = {

    idle: {

      background: "#222",

      border: "1px solid #444",

      boxShadow: "none"
    },

    pending: {

      background: "#222",

      border: "1px solid #444",

      boxShadow: "none"
    },

    running: {

      background: "#16333a",

      border: "2px solid #4deeea",

      boxShadow:
        "0 0 20px rgba(77,238,234,0.8)"
    },

    success: {

      background: "#18361f",

      border: "2px solid #57ff89",

      boxShadow:
        "0 0 20px rgba(87,255,137,0.8)"
    },

    error: {

      background: "#3a1616",

      border: "2px solid #ff5c5c",

      boxShadow:
        "0 0 20px rgba(255,92,92,0.8)"
    }
  };

  const currentStyle =
    styles[state] || styles.idle;

  console.log("🧩 ACTION NODE:", data);

  return (

    <>

      {/* INPUT HANDLE */}
      <Handle
        type="target"
        position={Position.Top}
        style={{
          background: "#4deeea",
          width: 10,
          height: 10,
          border: "2px solid #fff"
        }}
      />

      <div
        style={{

          padding: 10,

          color: "#fff",

          borderRadius: 8,

          minWidth: 220,

          transform:

            state === "running"
              ? "scale(1.03)"
              : "scale(1)",

          transition: "all 0.25s ease",

          ...currentStyle
        }}
      >

        {/* HEADER */}
        <div
          style={{

            marginBottom: 10,

            fontWeight: "bold",

            color:

              state === "running"
                ? "#4deeea"

              : state === "success"
                ? "#57ff89"

              : state === "error"
                ? "#ff5c5c"

              : "#fff"
          }}
        >
          ⚙️ {config.label}
        </div>

        {/* SELECT ACTION */}
        <select
          value={action}
          onChange={(e) =>
            changeAction(e.target.value)
          }
          style={{

            width: "100%",

            marginBottom: 10,

            padding: 6,

            borderRadius: 6,

            background: "#111",

            color: "#fff",

            border: "1px solid #555"
          }}
        >
          {Object.entries(ACTIONS).map(
            ([key, a]) => (

              <option
                key={key}
                value={key}
              >
                {a.label}
              </option>
            )
          )}
        </select>

        {/* DYNAMIC INPUTS */}
        <div>

          {config.fields.map((f) => (

            <input
              key={f.name}

              placeholder={f.label}

              value={
                data.params?.[f.name] || ""
              }

              onChange={(e) =>
                updateField(
                  f.name,
                  e.target.value
                )
              }

              style={{

                display: "block",

                marginTop: 5,

                width: "100%",

                padding: 6,

                borderRadius: 5,

                border: "1px solid #555",

                background: "#111",

                color: "#fff"
              }}
            />

          ))}

        </div>

        {/* RETRY + TIMEOUT */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>

          <input
            type="number"

            placeholder="Retries"

            value={data.retry || 0}

            onChange={(e) =>

              data.onChange(id, {

                ...data,

                retry: Number(e.target.value)
              })
            }

            style={{

              width: "100%",

              marginBottom: 5,

              padding: 6,

              borderRadius: 5,

              border: "1px solid #555",

              background: "#111",

              color: "#fff"
            }}
          />

          <input
            type="number"

            placeholder="Timeout (sec)"

            value={data.timeout || 30}

            onChange={(e) =>

              data.onChange(id, {

                ...data,

                timeout: Number(e.target.value)
              })
            }

            style={{

              width: "100%",

              padding: 6,

              borderRadius: 5,

              border: "1px solid #555",

              background: "#111",

              color: "#fff"
            }}
          />

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* OUTPUT HANDLE */}
      <Handle
        type="source"
        position={Position.Bottom}
        style={{
          background: "#57ff89",
          width: 10,
          height: 10,
          border: "2px solid #fff"
        }}
      />

    </>
  );
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/nodes/ConditionNode.jsx

import { Handle, Position } from "reactflow"

export default function ConditionNode({ data }) {

  return (
    <div style={{
      background:"#222",
      color:"#fff",
      padding:12,
      borderRadius:10,
      minWidth:180,
      border:"2px solid #ffaa00"
    }}>
      <strong>IF</strong>
      <div style={{fontSize:12, marginTop:6}}>
        {data.if_action} {data.operator} {data.compare}
      </div>

      {/* entrada */}
      <Handle type="target" position={Position.Top} />

      {/* salida THEN */}
      <Handle
        type="source"
        position={Position.Left}
        id="then"
        style={{ background:"#00cc66" }}
      />

      {/* salida ELSE */}
      <Handle
        type="source"
        position={Position.Right}
        id="else"
        style={{ background:"#ff4444" }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/pages/Builder.jsx

import ReactFlow, {
  Background,
  Controls,
  MiniMap,
  addEdge,
  applyNodeChanges,
  applyEdgeChanges
} from "reactflow";

import "reactflow/dist/style.css";

import {
  useState,
  useCallback,
  useEffect
} from "react";

import ActionNode from "../nodes/ActionNode";

import { getAuthHeaders } from "../services/auth";

const nodeTypes = {
  action: ActionNode
};

export default function Builder() {

  // =========================================================
  // STATE
  // =========================================================
  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState([]);
  const [edges, setEdges] = useState([]);

  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState("");
  const [missionGoal, setMissionGoal] = useState("");

  const [logs, setLogs] = useState([]);

  const [currentJobId, setCurrentJobId] =
    useState(null);

  const [generatedSteps, setGeneratedSteps] =
    useState([]);

  const [savedWorkflows, setSavedWorkflows] =
    useState([]);

  const [workflowName, setWorkflowName] =
    useState("");

  const [reactFlowInstance, setReactFlowInstance] =
    useState(null);

  const [executions, setExecutions] =
    useState([]);

  const [operatorGoal, setOperatorGoal] =
  useState("");  

  // =========================================================
  // LIVE STATE
  // =========================================================
  const [nodeStates, setNodeStates] =
    useState({});

  const [activeAction, setActiveAction] =
    useState(null);

  const [progress, setProgress] =
    useState(0);

  const [stepInfo, setStepInfo] =
    useState({
      completed: 0,
      total: 0
    });

  // =========================================================
  // LOAD WORKFLOWS
  // =========================================================
  useEffect(() => {

    loadWorkflows();

  }, []);

  // =========================================================
  // WEBSOCKET
  // =========================================================
  useEffect(() => {

    const ws = new WebSocket(
      "ws://127.0.0.1:8010/ws"
    );

    ws.onopen = () => {

      console.log("🟢 WS conectado");
    };

    ws.onmessage = (event) => {

      const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);

      console.log("📡 WS EVENT:", msg);

      // =====================================================
      // JOB LOGS
      // =====================================================
      if (
        msg.type === "job_log" &&
        msg.job_id === currentJobId
      ) {

        setLogs(prev => [
          ...prev,
          msg.message
        ]);

        // ===================================================
        // NODE STATES
        // ===================================================
        if (
          msg.node_id &&
          msg.status
        ) {

          setNodeStates(prev => ({

            ...prev,

            [msg.node_id]: msg.status
          }));
        }

        // ===================================================
        // ACTIVE ACTION
        // ===================================================
        if (
          msg.action &&
          msg.status === "running"
        ) {

          setActiveAction(msg.action);
        }

        // ===================================================
        // PROGRESS
        // ===================================================
        if (
          typeof msg.progress === "number"
        ) {

          setProgress(msg.progress);

          setStepInfo({
            completed:
              msg.completed_steps || 0,

            total:
              msg.total_steps || 0
          });
        }
      }

      // =====================================================
      // APPROVAL
      // =====================================================
      if (
        msg.type === "approval_required"
      ) {

        const ok = window.confirm(
          msg.message
        );

        if (ok) {

          fetch(
            `http://localhost:8010/approvals/${msg.job_id}`,
            {
              method: "POST",

              headers: {
                ...getAuthHeaders()
              }
            }
          );
        }
      }
    };

    ws.onerror = (err) => {

      console.log(
        "⚠️ WS error",
        err
      );
    };

    ws.onclose = () => {

      console.log("⚪ WS cerrado");
    };

    return () => {

      ws.close();
    };

  }, [currentJobId]);

  // =========================================================
  // UPDATE NODE
  // =========================================================
  const updateNodeData = (
    id,
    newData
  ) => {

    setNodes((nds) =>
      nds.map((n) =>
        n.id === id
          ? {
              ...n,

              data: {
                ...newData,
                onChange: updateNodeData
              }
            }
          : n
      )
    );
  };

  // =========================================================
  // ADD NODE
  // =========================================================
  const addNode = () => {

    const id = String(Date.now());

    const newNode = {

      id,

      type: "action",

      position: {
        x: 250,
        y: 250
      },

      data: {

        action: "create_folder",

        params: {},

        onChange: updateNodeData,

        nodeState: "pending"
      }
    };

    setNodes(prev => [
      ...prev,
      newNode
    ]);

    setTimeout(() => {

      reactFlowInstance?.fitView();

    }, 100);
  };

  // =========================================================
  // FLOW EVENTS
  // =========================================================
  const onNodesChange = useCallback(

    (changes) => {

      setNodes((nds) =>
        applyNodeChanges(
          changes,
          nds
        )
      );
    },

    []
  );

  const onEdgesChange = useCallback(

    (changes) => {

      setEdges((eds) =>
        applyEdgeChanges(
          changes,
          eds
        )
      );
    },

    []
  );

  const onConnect = (params) => {

    const label = window.prompt(
      "Tipo conexión: true / false",
      "true"
    );

    setEdges((eds) =>
      addEdge(
        {
          ...params,

          label:
            label || "true"
        },
        eds
      )
    );
  };

  // =========================================================
  // RUN WORKFLOW
  // =========================================================
  const runWorkflow = async () => {

    if (
      !nodes ||
      nodes.length === 0
    ) {

      alert(
        "No hay nodos para ejecutar"
      );

      return;
    }

    setLogs([]);
    setNodeStates({});
    setProgress(0);

    setStepInfo({
      completed: 0,
      total: nodes.length
    });

    try {

      const res = await fetch(
        "http://localhost:8010/jobs/run",
        {
          method: "POST",

          headers: {
            "Content-Type":
              "application/json",

            ...getAuthHeaders()
          },

          body: JSON.stringify({
            nodes,
            edges
          })
        }
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      console.log(
        "🔥 JOB RESPONSE:",
        data
      );

      if (!data.job_id) {

        alert(
          "Error ejecutando workflow"
        );

        return;
      }

      setCurrentJobId(
        data.job_id
      );

      setTimeout(() => {

        loadExecutions(
          data.job_id
        );

      }, 1000);

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // =========================================================
  // RUN AI
  // =========================================================
  const runAI = async () => {

    try {

      const res = await fetch(
        "http://localhost:8010/ai-builder/generate",
        {
          method: "POST",

          headers: {
            "Content-Type":
              "application/json",

            ...getAuthHeaders()
          },

          body: JSON.stringify({
            prompt
          })
        }
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      console.log(
        "🧠 IA RESPONSE:",
        data
      );

      if (
        !data.steps ||
        data.steps.length === 0
      ) {

        alert(
          "IA no generó pasos"
        );

        return;
      }

      setGeneratedSteps(
        data.steps
      );

      const newNodes =
        data.steps.map(
          (step, i) => ({

            id:
              `${Date.now()}-${i}`,

            type: "action",

            position: {
              x: 250,
              y: i * 160
            },

            data: {

              action:
                step.action,

              params:
                step.value || {},

              nodeState:
                "pending",

              onChange:
                updateNodeData
            }
          })
        );

      setNodes(newNodes);

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // =========================================================
  // SAVE WORKFLOW
  // =========================================================
  const saveWorkflow = async () => {

    if (!workflowName) {

      alert(
        "Nombre requerido"
      );

      return;
    }

    try {

      await fetch(
        "http://localhost:8010/workflows/save",
        {
          method: "POST",

          headers: {
            "Content-Type":
              "application/json",

            ...getAuthHeaders()
          },

          body: JSON.stringify({

            name:
              workflowName,

            nodes,

            edges
          })
        }
      );

      alert(
        "Workflow guardado 🚀"
      );

      loadWorkflows();

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // =========================================================
  // LOAD WORKFLOWS
  // =========================================================
  const loadWorkflows = async () => {

    try {

      const res = await fetch(
        "http://localhost:8010/workflows",
        {
          headers: {
            ...getAuthHeaders()
          }
        }
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setSavedWorkflows(data);

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // =========================================================
  // LOAD WORKFLOW
  // =========================================================
  const loadWorkflow = async (
    workflowId
  ) => {

    try {

      const res = await fetch(
        `http://localhost:8010/workflows/${workflowId}`,
        {
          headers: {
            ...getAuthHeaders()
          }
        }
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      const hydratedNodes =
        data.nodes.map(n => ({

          ...n,

          data: {

            ...n.data,

            onChange:
              updateNodeData,

            nodeState:
              nodeStates[n.id]
              || "idle"
          }
        }));

      setNodes(
        hydratedNodes
      );

      setEdges(
        data.edges || []
      );

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // =========================================================
  // LOAD EXECUTIONS
  // =========================================================
  const loadExecutions = async (
    jobId
  ) => {

    try {

      const res = await fetch(
        `http://localhost:8010/executions/${jobId}`,
        {
          headers: {
            ...getAuthHeaders()
          }
        }
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setExecutions(data);

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // =========================================================
  // RUN MISSION
  // =========================================================
  const runMission = async () => {

    try {

      const res = await fetch(
        "http://localhost:8010/missions/run",
        {
          method: "POST",

          headers: {
            "Content-Type":
              "application/json",

            ...getAuthHeaders()
          },

          body: JSON.stringify({
            goal: missionGoal
          })
        }
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      console.log(
        "🚀 MISSION:",
        data
      );

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // =========================================================
  // NODES STATE
  // =========================================================
  const nodesWithState =
    nodes.map((n) => ({

      ...n,

      data: {

        ...n.data,

        nodeState:
          nodeStates[n.id]
          || "pending"
      }
    }));

  // =========================================================
  // EDGES STATE
  // =========================================================
  const edgesWithState =
    edges.map((e) => {

      const sourceNode =
        nodes.find(
          n => n.id === e.source
        );

      const isActive =
        sourceNode?.data?.action
        === activeAction;

      return {

        ...e,

        animated: isActive,

        style: {

          stroke:
            isActive
              ? "#4deeea"
              : "#555",

          strokeWidth:
            isActive
              ? 3
              : 1.5
        }
      };
    });

  const runOperator = async () => {

  const res = await fetch(

    "http://localhost:8000/operator/run",

    {
      method: "POST",

      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        ...getAuthHeaders()
      },

      body: JSON.stringify({
        goal: operatorGoal
      })
    }
  );

  const data = await res.json();

  console.log(
    "🧠 OPERATOR:",
    data
  );
};  

  // =========================================================
  // UI
  // =========================================================
  return (

  <div
    style={{
      width: "100vw",
      height: "100vh",
      background: "#0d1117",
      overflow: "hidden"
    }}
  >

    {/* ================================================= */}
    {/* TOP BAR */}
    {/* ================================================= */}
    <div
      style={{
        position: "fixed",

        top: 0,
        left: 0,
        right: 0,

        height: 72,

        zIndex: 100,

        display: "flex",

        alignItems: "center",

        gap: 10,

        padding: "0 16px",

        background: "rgba(10,10,10,0.96)",

        borderBottom: "1px solid #222",

        backdropFilter: "blur(10px)"
      }}
    >

      <button onClick={addNode}>
        + Nodo
      </button>

      <button onClick={runWorkflow}>
        ▶ Ejecutar
      </button>

      <input
        placeholder="Nombre workflow"
        value={workflowName}
        onChange={(e) =>
          setWorkflowName(e.target.value)
        }
        style={{
          width: 180
        }}
      />

      <button onClick={saveWorkflow}>
        💾 Guardar
      </button>

      <input
        placeholder="Ej: ordenar PDFs"
        value={prompt}
        onChange={(e) =>
          setPrompt(e.target.value)
        }
        style={{
          width: 220
        }}
      />

      <button onClick={runAI}>
        ✨ AI
      </button>

      <input
        placeholder="Mission Goal"
        value={missionGoal}
        onChange={(e) =>
          setMissionGoal(e.target.value)
        }
        style={{
          width: 260
        }}
      />

      <button onClick={runMission}>
        🚀 Run Mission
      </button>

    </div>

    <div
  style={{
    position: "absolute",
    top: 70,
    left: 10,
    zIndex: 20
  }}
>

  <input
    placeholder="Operator Goal"
    value={operatorGoal}
    onChange={(e) =>
      setOperatorGoal(
        e.target.value
      )
    }
  />

  <button
    onClick={runOperator}
  >
    🧠 Run Operator
  </button>

</div>

    {/* ================================================= */}
    {/* LEFT SIDEBAR */}
    {/* ================================================= */}
    <div
      style={{
        position: "fixed",

        top: 82,
        left: 10,

        bottom: 10,

        width: 280,

        zIndex: 50,

        background: "#111",

        border: "1px solid #222",

        borderRadius: 12,

        overflow: "hidden",

        display: "flex",

        flexDirection: "column"
      }}
    >

      <div
        style={{
          padding: 14,

          borderBottom: "1px solid #222",

          fontWeight: "bold",

          color: "#fff"
        }}
      >
        📂 Workflows
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,

          overflowY: "auto",

          padding: 12
        }}
      >

        {savedWorkflows.length === 0 && (

          <div
            style={{
              color: "#888"
            }}
          >
            No hay workflows
          </div>
        )}

        {savedWorkflows.map((wf) => (

          <div
            key={wf.id}
            style={{
              background: "#1a1a1a",

              borderRadius: 10,

              padding: 10,

              marginBottom: 10
            }}
          >

            <div
              style={{
                color: "#fff",

                marginBottom: 8
              }}
            >
              {wf.name}
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() =>
                loadWorkflow(wf.id)
              }
            >
              ▶ Load
            </button>

          </div>
        ))}

      </div>

    </div>

    {/* ================================================= */}
    {/* RIGHT SIDEBAR */}
    {/* ================================================= */}
    <div
      style={{
        position: "fixed",

        top: 82,
        right: 10,

        bottom: 10,

        width: 340,

        zIndex: 50,

        display: "flex",

        flexDirection: "column",

        gap: 12
      }}
    >

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* EXECUTION PANEL */}
      {/* ============================================= */}
      <div
        style={{
          background: "#111",

          border: "1px solid #222",

          borderRadius: 12,

          overflow: "hidden",

          minHeight: 250,

          maxHeight: 320,

          display: "flex",

          flexDirection: "column"
        }}
      >

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 14,

            borderBottom: "1px solid #222",

            fontWeight: "bold",

            color: "#fff"
          }}
        >
          📜 Execution Timeline
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            flex: 1,

            overflowY: "auto",

            padding: 12
          }}
        >

          {executions.map((e, i) => (

            <div
              key={i}
              style={{
                padding: 10,

                marginBottom: 10,

                borderRadius: 8,

                background:
                  e.status === "success"
                    ? "#13361b"
                    : e.status === "error"
                    ? "#3a1616"
                    : "#222"
              }}
            >

              <div
                style={{
                  color: "#fff"
                }}
              >
                ⚙️ {e.action}
              </div>

              <div
                style={{
                  color: "#aaa",

                  marginTop: 4
                }}
              >
                Status: {e.status}
              </div>

              <div
                style={{
                  fontSize: 12,

                  opacity: 0.7,

                  marginTop: 6,

                  color: "#777"
                }}
              >
                {e.created_at}
              </div>

            </div>
          ))}

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* PROGRESS */}
      {/* ============================================= */}
      <div
        style={{
          background: "#0b0f14",

          border: "1px solid #188686",

          borderRadius: 12,

          padding: 14
        }}
      >

        <div
          style={{
            color: "#4deeea",

            marginBottom: 10
          }}
        >
          🚀 Progreso:
          {" "}
          {stepInfo.completed}
          /
          {stepInfo.total}
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            width: "100%",

            height: 10,

            background: "#222",

            borderRadius: 999
          }}
        >

          <div
            style={{
              width: `${progress}%`,

              height: "100%",

              background: "#4deeea",

              borderRadius: 999,

              transition: "width 0.3s ease"
            }}
          />

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* LOGS */}
      {/* ============================================= */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,

          background: "#0b0f14",

          border: "1px solid #188686",

          borderRadius: 12,

          overflow: "hidden",

          display: "flex",

          flexDirection: "column"
        }}
      >

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 14,

            borderBottom: "1px solid #133",

            color: "#4deeea",

            fontWeight: "bold"
          }}
        >
          🧠 Runtime Logs
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            flex: 1,

            overflowY: "auto",

            padding: 14,

            color: "#4deeea"
          }}
        >

          {logs.length === 0 && (

            <div>
              Esperando ejecución...
            </div>
          )}

          {logs.map((log, i) => (

            <div
              key={i}
              style={{
                marginBottom: 8
              }}
            >
              • {log}
            </div>

          ))}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>

    {/* ================================================= */}
    {/* FLOW */}
    {/* ================================================= */}
    <div
      style={{
        position: "absolute",

        top: 72,
        left: 0,
        right: 0,
        bottom: 0
      }}
    >

      <ReactFlow
        nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
        nodes={nodesWithState}
        edges={edgesWithState}
        onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
        onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
        onConnect={onConnect}
        onInit={setReactFlowInstance}
        deleteKeyCode={["Backspace", "Delete"]}
        fitView
      >
        <MiniMap />
        <Controls />
        <Background />
      </ReactFlow>

    </div>

  </div>
);
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { authFetch } from "../services/auth";
import Navbar from "../components/Navbar";

import JobsPanel from "../features/jobs/JobsPanel";
import MissionControl from "../features/system/MissionControl";
import RealtimePanel from "../features/jobs/RealtimePanel";

export default function Dashboard() {

  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    authFetch("http://localhost:8010/metrics")
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(setMetrics);
  }, []);

  if (!metrics) return <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Cargando sistema...</div>;

  return (
    <div>

      {/* 🔥 NAVBAR */}
      <Navbar />

      <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>

        {/* 🔥 NEXUS CONTROL (RECUPERADO) */}
        <h1 style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>🧠 Nexus Control</h1>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 20 }}>
          <Card title="Jobs activos" value={metrics.active_jobs} />
          <Card title="Errores" value={metrics.counters?.errors || 0} />
          <Card
            title="Circuit"
            value={metrics.circuit_state?.state || "unknown"}
          />
        </div>

        {/* 🔥 SYSTEM CONTROL */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 30 }}>
          <MissionControl />
        </div>

        {/* 🔥 JOBS */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 30 }}>
          <JobsPanel />
        </div>

        <RealtimePanel />

        {/* 🔥 DEBUG / LOG */}
        <h2 style={{ marginTop: 30 }}>Actividad</h2>

        <pre style={{
          background: "#111",
          color: "#0f0",
          padding: 10,
          borderRadius: 8
        }}>
          {JSON.stringify(metrics, null, 2)}
        </pre>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Card({ title, value }) {

  const display =
    typeof value === "object"
      ? JSON.stringify(value)
      : value;

  return (
    <div style={{
      background: "#1a1a1a",
      padding: 20,
      borderRadius: 10,
      minWidth: 150
    }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#aaa" }}>{title}</div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 22 }}>{display}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
