import { useState } from "react";

const API = "http://127.0.0.1:8010";

export default function Login() {

  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const login = async () => {

    if (!username || !password) {
      setError("Completá usuario y contraseña");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {

      const res = await fetch(`${API}/auth/login`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json();
        throw new Error(err.detail || "Error en login");
      }

      const data = await res.json();

      // guardar sesión
      localStorage.setItem("token", data.access_token);
      localStorage.setItem("user", JSON.stringify(data.user));

      // redirigir
      window.location.href = "/";

    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
      gap: 10,
      maxWidth: 300,
      margin: "100px auto"
    }}>

      <h2>Login Nexus</h2>

      <input
        placeholder="Usuario"
        value={username}
        onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
      />

      <input
        placeholder="Contraseña"
        type="password"
        value={password}
        onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
      />

      <button onClick={login} disabled={loading}>
        {loading ? "Ingresando..." : "Login"}
      </button>

      {error && (
        <div style={{ color: "red", fontSize: 14 }}>
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/pages/Register.jsx

import { useState } from "react";

export default function Register() {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const register = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const res = await fetch("http://localhost:8010/auth/register", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          username,
          password,
          tenant_id: "default", // 🔥 importante para tu sistema multi-tenant
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.detail || "Error al registrarse");
        return;
      }

      // ✔ opcional: autologin después de registrar
      if (data.token) {
        localStorage.setItem("token", data.token);
        window.location.href = "/";
      } else {
        window.location.href = "/login";
      }

    } catch (err) {
      setError("Error de conexión con el servidor");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 40, maxWidth: 300 }}>

      <h2>Crear cuenta</h2>

      <input
        placeholder="Usuario"
        value={username}
        onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
      />

      <input
        placeholder="Password"
        type="password"
        value={password}
        onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
      />

      <button onClick={register} disabled={loading}>
        {loading ? "Creando..." : "Registrarse"}
      </button>

      {error && (
        <div style={{ color: "red", marginTop: 10 }}>
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { motion } from "framer-motion"
import { authFetch } from "../services/auth"

const API = "http://127.0.0.1:8010"

export default function Setup(){

  const [packs,setPacks]=useState([])
  const [loading,setLoading]=useState(false)

  /* ======================
     CARGAR PACKS REALES
  ====================== */

  useEffect(()=>{

    authFetch(`${API}/workflows/packs`)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(setPacks)
      .catch(()=>{
        setPacks([
          { slug:"hotel", name:"Hotel Edition", color:"#5b8cff" },
          { slug:"bar", name:"Bar Edition", color:"#7c5cff" },
          { slug:"office", name:"Office Edition", color:"#00b894" }
        ])
      })

  },[])

  /* ======================
     INSTALAR
  ====================== */

  const choose = async (slug)=>{

    try{
      setLoading(true)

      await authFetch(`${API}/setup/install`,{
        method:"POST",
        headers:{
          "Content-Type":"application/json"
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ slug })
      })

      window.location.reload()

    }catch(e){
      console.error(e)
      alert("Error instalando pack")
    }finally{
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  /* ====================== */

  return(
    <div style={styles.page}>

      {/* Glow fondo */}
      <div style={styles.glow}/>

      <motion.div
        initial={{opacity:0, scale:.95}}
        animate={{opacity:1, scale:1}}
        transition={{duration:.4}}
        style={styles.card}
      >

        <h1 style={styles.title}>Nexus</h1>
        <p style={styles.subtitle}>
          Elegí la edición para comenzar
        </p>

        <div style={styles.grid}>
          {packs.map(p=>(
            <button
              key={p.slug}
              disabled={loading}
              onClick={()=>choose(p.slug)}
              style={{
                ...styles.packBtn,
                background:p.color
              }}
            >
              ⚡ {p.name}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {loading && (
          <div style={styles.loading}>
            Instalando pack...
          </div>
        )}

      </motion.div>
    </div>
  )
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/pages/Users.jsx
===========================================================================


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { authFetch } from "../services/auth";

export default function Users() {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const fetchUsers = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await authFetch("http://localhost:8010/auth/users");
      const data = await res.json();
      setUsers(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Error cargando usuarios:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchUsers();
  }, []);

  const deleteUser = async (username) => {
    try {
      await authFetch(`http://localhost:8010/auth/users/${username}`, {
        method: "DELETE",
      });

      // refrescar lista
      fetchUsers();

    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Error eliminando usuario:", err);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Cargando usuarios...</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
      <h2>Usuarios</h2>

      {users.length === 0 && (
        <div>No hay usuarios</div>
      )}

      {users.map((u) => (
        <div
          key={u.username}
          style={{
            marginBottom: 10,
            padding: 10,
            background: "#1a1a1a",
            borderRadius: 8,
            display: "flex",
            justifyContent: "space-between",
            alignItems: "center"
          }}
        >
          <div>
            <b>{u.username}</b> ({u.role})
          </div>

          <button onClick={() => deleteUser(u.username)}>
            Eliminar
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

import { getAuthHeaders } from "./auth";

export async function generateWorkflow(prompt) {

  try {

    const res = await fetch("http://localhost:8010/ai-builder/generate", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        ...getAuthHeaders()
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ prompt })
    });

    const text = await res.text(); // 🔥 clave

    console.log("🧠 RAW RESPONSE:", text);

    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(text);
    }

    const data = JSON.parse(text);

    return data;

  } catch (err) {

    console.error("🔥 ERROR IA:", err);

    alert("ERROR IA:\n" + err.message);

    throw err;
  }
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: dashboard/src/services/auth.js
===========================================================================


export function authFetch(url, options = {}) {

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  // 🔴 No hay token → cortar todo
  if (!token) {
    window.location.href = "/login";
    return Promise.reject("No token");
  }

  return fetch(url, {
    ...options,
    headers: {
      ...(options.headers || {}),
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
    }
  })
  .then(res => {

    // 🔴 Token inválido o expirado
    if (res.status === 401) {
      localStorage.clear();
      window.location.href = "/login";
      return Promise.reject("Unauthorized");
    }

    return res;
  });
}

export function getAuthHeaders() {

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  if (!token) {
    throw new Error("No token");
  }

  return {
    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
  };
}

import { getAuthHeaders } from "./auth";

export async function runWorkflow(steps) {

  const res = await fetch("http://localhost:8010/jobs/run", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      ...getAuthHeaders()
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      name: "builder-workflow",
      steps
    })
  });

  return res.json();
}

let socket;

export function connectWS(onMessage) {
  socket = new WebSocket("ws://localhost:8010/ws");

  socket.onmessage = (event) => {
    const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
    onMessage(data);
  };
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: desktop/main.js

const { app, BrowserWindow, Tray, Menu, globalShortcut } = require("electron")
const path = require("path")
const { spawn } = require("child_process")

let win
let tray

// 🔹 rutas seguras
const frontendPath = path.join(__dirname, "../frontend/dist/index.html")
const splashPath = path.join(__dirname, "splash.html")
const backendPath = path.join(__dirname, "../backend/dist/nexus-backend.exe")


function createWindow() {

  win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 800,
    height: 500,
    show: false,
    frame: false,
    transparent: true,
    alwaysOnTop: true,
    skipTaskbar: true,
    resizable: false,
    center: true,
    webPreferences: {
      nodeIntegration: false
    }
  })

  // 🔥 primero splash
  win.loadFile(splashPath)

  // 🔥 después carga app real (delay pequeño)
  setTimeout(() => {
    win.loadFile(frontendPath)
  }, 1200)
}


function toggle() {
  if (win.isVisible()) win.hide()
  else {
    win.show()
    win.focus()
  }
}


app.whenReady().then(() => {

  createWindow()

  // 🔥 backend exe (rutas seguras)
  spawn(backendPath, [], {
    detached: true,
    stdio: "ignore"
  })

  // 🔥 autostart windows
  app.setLoginItemSettings({
    openAtLogin: true
  })

  // 🔥 tray
  tray = new Tray(path.join(__dirname, "icon.ico"))

  const menu = Menu.buildFromTemplate([
    { label: "Abrir Nexus", click: toggle },
    { type: "separator" },
    { label: "Salir", click: () => app.quit() }
  ])

  tray.setToolTip("Nexus IA")
  tray.setContextMenu(menu)

  // 🔥 hotkey global
  globalShortcut.register("Control+Space", toggle)
})


// 🔥 limpieza correcta
app.on("will-quit", () => {
  globalShortcut.unregisterAll()
})
